Kaggriculture master leaderboard

All 184,320 games in the panels below passed validation, with zero invalid, missing or duplicate cells. The packages use the same pinned official 1.32.7 game rules, 16 opponents and both seats.

This update adds 61,440 Pro8 games to the 122,880 original games. The original results were preserved and every original statistic reconciled exactly. The Pro8 cohort includes A08 r11, A06 r6 and A06 r12 calendar from source commit d653253. The holdout is retired: Pro8 uses it as a reused benchmark.

Bold marks the highest observed win rate among candidates in that comparison. Exact ties are all bold. This is a numerical ranking, not a claim of statistical certainty. Draws count as zero wins. Confidence intervals resample whole seeds; repeated subgroup comparisons are exploratory. Mean cash and margin add context but do not determine the winner.

Representative

These 256 seeds were drawn uniformly from the eligible default 31-bit seed domain before their economic conditions were measured. This panel estimates typical seed performance against the fixed opponent mix. It does not estimate the live Kaggle opponent distribution. Once inspected, it becomes development evidence.

The panel contains 256 seeds and 8,192 games per candidate: all 16 opponents in both seats.

The highest observed aggregate rate is v37 feed reserve at 90.84%. This supports a claim about average performance on eligible random seeds against this fixed roster. v37 feed reserve differs from Day9 by +7.10 percentage points (95% paired interval +4.16 to +10.24 pp). That interval excludes zero for this comparison. Against AFS R2 directly, v37 wins 28.71% and Day9 wins 94.73%. An aggregate ranking can therefore differ sharply from a specific matchup.

A08 r11 wins 76.56% overall and 60.94% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -7.18 pp (95% interval -10.12 to -4.16 pp). A06 r6 wins 80.75% overall and 90.04% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -2.99 pp (95% interval -6.02 to +0.04 pp). A06 r12 calendar wins 77.84% overall and 91.02% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -5.90 pp (95% interval -9.29 to -2.47 pp).

Stress

These 128 seeds were selected from a separate 4,096-seed pool to cover extremes and contrasts in 26 economic coordinates, all eight reference first-shop types, and 12 contrasting cells. Selection used no candidate outcomes. This panel tests coverage of selected conditions; its average is not a population estimate, and extreme conditions need not make a policy lose more often. Reference shop labels use a PASS/PASS controller. Actual shops and prices depend on both players’ actions.

The panel contains 128 seeds and 4,096 games per candidate: all 16 opponents in both seats.

The highest observed aggregate rate is v37 feed reserve at 90.26%. This describes the selected economic conditions. A higher rate here than on representative seeds does not by itself prove greater robustness. v37 feed reserve differs from Day9 by +3.32 percentage points (95% paired interval -0.20 to +7.15 pp). That interval includes zero, so a lead over Day9 remains uncertain. Against AFS R2 directly, v37 wins 22.66% and Day9 wins 94.92%. An aggregate ranking can therefore differ sharply from a specific matchup.

A08 r11 wins 78.49% overall and 64.45% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -8.45 pp (95% interval -12.06 to -4.81 pp). A06 r6 wins 79.98% overall and 87.50% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -6.96 pp (95% interval -11.18 to -2.88 pp). A06 r12 calendar wins 77.47% overall and 86.72% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -9.47 pp (95% interval -13.79 to -5.20 pp).

Timing reflects the recorded host and concurrent workload. The original six ran locally; Pro8 ran on local and WRX90 CPU workers. These timings are not a matched speed comparison or a Kaggle runtime guarantee. Optional terminal debug errors are separate from action and terminal validation.

Holdout

These 256 independently drawn seeds excluded known campaign history, representative seeds and the full stress selection pool. The original six candidates, settings and analysis were frozen before their comparison, and every candidate received this panel. That was their one check on unseen seeds. The panel was then published and retired. The three Pro8 agents reuse these same seeds as a known benchmark; their results do not provide fresh holdout confirmation. The audit cannot recover deleted, unsaved or out-of-scope history. Future confirmation needs a new independent set.

The panel contains 256 seeds and 8,192 games per candidate: all 16 opponents in both seats.

The highest observed aggregate rate is v37 feed reserve at 91.22%. The observed leader agrees with the representative panel. For Pro8 this measures performance on the reused benchmark; it cannot establish generalization to a fresh holdout. v37 feed reserve differs from Day9 by +8.20 percentage points (95% paired interval +5.14 to +11.45 pp). That interval excludes zero for this comparison. Against AFS R2 directly, v37 wins 30.08% and Day9 wins 96.09%. An aggregate ranking can therefore differ sharply from a specific matchup.

A08 r11 wins 76.72% overall and 64.06% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -6.30 pp (95% interval -9.22 to -3.44 pp). A06 r6 wins 75.88% overall and 86.52% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -7.14 pp (95% interval -10.56 to -3.70 pp). A06 r12 calendar wins 76.23% overall and 88.87% against AFS R2. Its paired difference from Day9 is -6.79 pp (95% interval -10.14 to -3.54 pp).

Timing reflects the recorded host and concurrent workload. The original six ran locally; Pro8 ran on local and WRX90 CPU workers. These timings are not a matched speed comparison or a Kaggle runtime guarantee. Optional terminal debug errors are separate from action and terminal validation.

Methods and evidence

The same frozen candidate files and opponent versions ran on each panel. Each game completed 719 transitions. The analysis uses 4,000 common seed bootstrap draws per panel, preserving every opponent/seat game for a seed. Paired intervals compare matched seeds; subtracting separate interval endpoints would not produce a paired interval. Bold uses exact wins/games before rounding.

The Pro8 agents ran through their supplied create_agent entry, codec, configuration and native library. Both hosts passed all 384 verification cases before primary play, with exact agreement on actions, terminal cash, shops and market summaries: 1,536 full verification games and 1,105,920 official observation checks. These verification games are excluded from win rates. Frozen, disjoint assignments split the 61,440 new games across two hosts.

The same seed bootstrap and analysis rules apply to both cohorts. Hash-pinned packages make this comparison reproducible; later edits require a new row and run receipt. The public-parent v37 policy does not establish the team’s Three-Layer architecture goal. No result on this page changes the champion or submits to Kaggle.
